# EU law in Romanian: regulations and directives in force (Official Journal text): 2026

910 acts, 910 versions. Each act lives in a folder named by its official identifier, `<kind>/<year>/<number>/` (Legea nr. 31/1990 is `lege/1990/31/`); its README.md lists its articles and the decisions made by hand. Dates are in-force days. Generated on every publish; the same list is in [2026.json](2026.json).
